1. WinGo analysis mein use hone wale mathematical methods
1. Frequency Analysis
Pichhle 50, 100, 500 ya 1,000 results mein har number kitni baar aaya, ye count karna.
Example: 0 = 8 baar, 1 = 12 baar, 2 = 9 baar.
Kaam: Number distribution aur unusual frequencies dekhna.
2. Probability & Percentage
Kisi category ki historical frequency ko percentage mein badalna.
Formula: 
Kaam: GREEN/RED, colour ya number ki historical frequency compare karna.
3. Moving Window / Recent Trend
Sirf latest 10, 20 ya 50 rounds ka analysis karke usse longer history se compare karna.
Kaam: Recent distribution mein badlav identify karna; agla result guarantee nahi karna.
Streak & Transition Analysis
Jaise GREEN → GREEN → RED, ya kitni baar GREEN ke baad RED aaya.
Kaam: Result sequences ka pattern measure karna. Independent draws mein streak khud se reversal ki guarantee nahi hoti.
5. Conditional Probability / Markov Analysis
Historical data mein check karna ki pichhle result ya pichhle do results ke baad kaunsa result kitni baar aaya.
Kaam: Sequence dependence test karna; sirf correlation milna kaafi nahi, out-of-sample validation bhi chahiye.
6. Statistical Significance
Chi-square test, confidence intervals aur expected frequency se check karna ki observed pattern random variation se alag hai ya nahi.
Kaam: False patterns ko genuine evidence samajhne ki galti kam karna.
7. Backtesting & Accuracy Validation
Purane data par model test karna, lekin har prediction ke waqt sirf us samay available data use karna. Phir unseen results par test repeat karna.
Kaam: Pata karna ki method simple baseline se behtar hai bhi ya nahi.
Step 2 — Data Validation
Duplicate periods, missing results aur incorrect records check karna
Step 3 — Statistical Features
Frequency, recent trend, streaks, transitions aur number distribution
Step 4 — Model & Probability
Baseline probability ke saath candidate predictions compare karna
Step 5 — Validation & Output
Backtest, measured accuracy, probability estimate aur prediction status

    'ZIGZAG_BLOCK_S': ['Zigzag→RED Block 🌀', '🌀 Zigzag merged into RED block!'],
    'PRERRURE_B': ['GREEN Pressure ⬆️', '⬆️ GREEN will force through!'],
    'PRERRURE_S': ['RED Pressure ⬇️', '⬇️ RED will break through!'],
    'REBOUND_B': ['GREEN Rebound ↩️', '↩️ GREEN rebounded — will hold!'],
    'REBOUND_S': ['RED Rebound ↩️', '↩️ RED rebounded — continues!'],
};

// ── Current prediction state ──
let currentPrediction = null;
let bunnyUpdateCount = 0;

// ── Helper: Convert history colour to R/G ──
function colourToType(result) {
    // Accept WinGo history objects or direct colour strings.
    const raw = typeof result === 'string'
        ? result
        : (result?.colour ?? result?.color ?? result?.colourName ?? result?.colorName);
    if (!raw) return null;
    const value = String(raw).toUpperCase();
    if (value.includes('RED')) return 'R';
    if (value.includes('GREEN')) return 'G';
    return null;
}

// ── Helper: Look up pattern in DB ──
function lookupPattern(pattern) {
    return CPU_PATTERN_DB[pattern] || null;
}

// ── Helper: Live adaptive detection (fallback) ──
function liveAdaptiveDetect(types) {
    if (types.length < 4) return null;
    const len = Math.min(types.length, 10);
    const slice = types.slice(0, len);
    const seq = slice.join('');

    // Try to find longest matching pattern in DB
    for (let l = Math.min(seq.length, 9); l >= 3; l--) {
        const sub = seq.substring(0, l);
        const hit = lookupPattern(sub);
        if (hit) {
            return {
                method: 'AI_LIVE',
                size: hit.next,
                conf: hit.conf
            };
        }
    }

    // Fallback: statistical
    const bCount = slice.filter(t => t === 'G').length;
    const total = slice.length;
    const bRatio = bCount / total;
    if (bRatio >= 0.7) {
        return { method: 'MATH', size: 'RED', conf: 65 + Math.floor(bRatio * 20) };
    } else if (bRatio <= 0.3) {
        return { method: 'MATH', size: 'GREEN', conf: 65 + Math.floor((1 - bRatio) * 20) };
    }
    return null;
}

// ── Main Bunny AI Engine ──
function runBunnyAI(historyNumbers) {
    if (!historyNumbers || historyNumbers.length < 3) return;
    bunnyUpdateCount++;

    const hist10 = historyNumbers.slice(0, 10);
    const deepHist = historyNumbers.slice(0, 500);
    const types = deepHist.map(n => colourToType(n)).filter(Boolean);

    // ── Update dots (safe DOM) ──
    const dotsEl = document.getElementById('bunnyDots');
    if (dotsEl) {
        dotsEl.innerHTML = hist10.map((n, i) => {
            const type = colourToType(n);
            const isGreen = type === 'G';
            const col = isGreen ? '#22c55e' : '#ef4444';
            const bg = isGreen ? 'rgba(34,197,94,0.15)' : 'rgba(239,68,68,0.15)';
            const label = type || '?';
            const opacity = 1 - (i * 0.07);
            return `<div style="width:20px;height:20px;border-radius:50%;background:${bg};border:1.5px solid ${col};display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-size:8px;font-weight:900;color:${col};opacity:${opacity};flex-shrink:0;">${label}</div>`;
        }).join('');
    }

    // ── Detect pattern ──
    let patKey = 'MULTI';
    let pred = currentPrediction ? currentPrediction.size : '--';
    let conf = currentPrediction ? currentPrediction.confidence : 70;

    // ── Streak detection ──
    let sk = 1;
    for (let i = 0; i < types.length - 1 && types[i] === types[i + 1]; i++) sk++;
    const cur = types[0];
    const opp = cur === 'G' ? 'R' : 'G';

    // ── Cycle detection ──
    let cycleFound = false;
    for (let cl = 2; cl <= 8; cl++) {
        if (types.length < cl * 2) continue;
        let ok = true;
        for (let j = 0; j < cl; j++) {
            if (types[j] !== types[j + cl]) { ok = false; break; }
        }
        if (ok) {
            patKey = 'CYCLE_' + cl;
            cycleFound = true;
            conf = cl >= 5 ? 96 : cl >= 3 ? 91 : 82;
            break;
        }
    }

    if (!cycleFound) {
        const b10 = types.slice(0, 10).filter(t => t === 'G').length;

        if (sk >= 6) {
            patKey = cur === 'G' ? 'DRAGON_G' : 'DRAGON_R';
            conf = Math.min(95, conf + 3);
            pred = cur === 'G' ? 'RED' : 'GREEN';
        } else if (sk === 5) {
            patKey = cur === 'G' ? '5x_STREAK_G' : '5x_STREAK_R';
            conf = Math.min(93, conf + 2);
            pred = cur === 'G' ? 'RED' : 'GREEN';
        } else if (sk >= 4) {
            patKey = '4x_STREAK_' + cur;
            conf = Math.min(92, conf + 1);
            pred = cur === 'G' ? 'RED' : 'GREEN';
        } else if (sk === 3) {
            const p6 = types.length >= 6 ? types.slice(0, 6).join('') : '';
            const p7 = types.length >= 7 ? types.slice(0, 7).join('') : '';
            const p8 = types.length >= 8 ? types.slice(0, 8).join('') : '';

            if (p8 === 'GGGRGRGR' || p7 === 'GGGRGRG' || p6 === 'GGGRGR') {
                patKey = 'GGGRGR_B';
                conf = 80;
                pred = 'RED';
            } else if (p8 === 'RRRGRGRG' || p7 === 'RRRGRGR' || p6 === 'RRRGRG') {
                patKey = 'GGGRGR_S';
                conf = 80;
                pred = 'GREEN';
            } else {
                patKey = '3x_STREAK_' + cur;
                conf = 78;
                pred = cur === 'G' ? 'RED' : 'GREEN';
            }
        } else if (sk === 2) {
            patKey = '2x_STREAK_' + cur;
            conf = 65;
            pred = cur === 'G' ? 'RED' : 'GREEN';
        } else {
            // ── Advanced pattern detection ──
            const seq6 = types.length >= 6 ? types.slice(0, 6).join('') : '';
            const seq7 = types.length >= 7 ? types.slice(0, 7).join('') : '';
            const seq8 = types.length >= 8 ? types.slice(0, 8).join('') : '';
            const seq9 = types.length >= 9 ? types.slice(0, 9).join('') : '';
            const seq10 = types.length >= 10 ? types.slice(0, 10).join('') : '';

            let found = false;

            // ── STUTTER / SINGLE BREAK ──
            if (seq8 === 'GGGGGGGR' || seq7 === 'GGGGGGR' || seq6 === 'GGGGGR') {
                patKey = 'STUTTER_G';
                conf = 86;
                pred = 'RED';
                found = true;
            } else if (seq8 === 'RRRRRRRG' || seq7 === 'RRRRRRG' || seq6 === 'RRRRRG') {
                patKey = 'STUTTER_R';
                conf = 86;
                pred = 'GREEN';
                found = true;
            } else if (seq6 === 'GGGGRG' || seq5 === 'GGGRG') {
                patKey = 'SINGLE_GREAK_G';
                conf = 79;
                pred = 'RED';
                found = true;
            } else if (seq6 === 'RRRRGR' || seq5 === 'RRRGR') {
                patKey = 'SINGLE_GREAK_R';
                conf = 79;
                pred = 'GREEN';
                found = true;
            }

            // ── TRAP ──
            if (!found && (seq6 === 'GGRRGG' || seq7 === 'GGRRGG' || seq8 === 'GGRRGG')) {
                patKey = 'TRAP_G';
                conf = 83;
                pred = 'RED';
                found = true;
            } else if (!found && (seq6 === 'RRGGRR' || seq7 === 'RRGGRR' || seq8 === 'RRGGRR')) {
                patKey = 'TRAP_R';
                conf = 83;
                pred = 'GREEN';
                found = true;
            }

            // ── SANDWICH ──
            if (!found && seq5 === 'GGRGG') {
                patKey = 'SANDWICH_B';
                conf = 71;
                pred = 'RED';
                found = true;
            } else if (!found && seq5 === 'RRGRR') {
                patKey = 'SANDWICH_S';
                conf = 71;
                pred = 'GREEN';
                found = true;
            }

            // ── PENDULUM ──
            if (!found && (seq7 === 'GGRRRGG' || seq8 === 'GGRRRGG')) {
                patKey = 'PENDULUM';
                conf = 78;
                pred = 'RED';
                found = true;
            } else if (!found && (seq7 === 'RRGGGRR' || seq8 === 'RRGGGRR')) {
                patKey = 'PENDULUM';
                conf = 78;
                pred = 'GREEN';
                found = true;
            }

            // ── TWIN PULSE ──
            if (!found && (seq6 === 'GRGGRG' || seq5 === 'GRGGR')) {
                patKey = 'TWIN_PULSE';
                conf = 68;
                pred = 'RED';
                found = true;
            } else if (!found && (seq6 === 'RGRRGR' || seq5 === 'RGRRG')) {
                patKey = 'TWIN_PULSE';
                conf = 68;
                pred = 'GREEN';
                found = true;
            }

            // ── WAVE ──
            if (!found && (seq6 === 'RGGGRR' || seq7 === 'RGGGRR')) {
                patKey = 'WAVE_UP';
                conf = 69;
                pred = 'GREEN';
                found = true;
            } else if (!found && (seq6 === 'GRRGG' || seq7 === 'GRRGG')) {
                patKey = 'WAVE_DOWN';
                conf = 69;
                pred = 'RED';
                found = true;
            }

            // ── MIRROR ──
            if (!found && seq6 === 'GGRRGG') {
                patKey = 'MIRROR';
                conf = 83;
                pred = 'RED';
                found = true;
            } else if (!found && seq6 === 'RRGGRR') {
                patKey = 'MIRROR';
                conf = 83;
                pred = 'GREEN';
                found = true;
            }

            // ── FADE ──
            if (!found && (seq6 === 'RGGGRR' || seq7 === 'RGGGRR')) {
                patKey = 'FADE_B';
                conf = 69;
                pred = 'GREEN';
                found = true;
            } else if (!found && (seq6 === 'GRRGG' || seq7 === 'GRRGG')) {
                patKey = 'FADE_S';
                conf = 69;
                pred = 'RED';
                found = true;
            }

            // ── CLUSTER JUMP ──
            if (!found && (seq7 === 'GGGGRRR' || seq8 === 'GGGGRRR')) {
                patKey = 'CLUSTER_JUMP';
                conf = 88;
                pred = 'RED';
                found = true;
            } else if (!found && (seq7 === 'RRRRGGG' || seq8 === 'RRRRGGG')) {
                patKey = 'CLUSTER_JUMP';
                conf = 88;
                pred = 'GREEN';
                found = true;
            }

            // ── ECHO ──
            if (!found && (seq6 === 'GRGRG' || seq7 === 'GRGRG')) {
                patKey = 'ECHO';
                conf = 65;
                pred = 'RED';
                found = true;
            } else if (!found && (seq6 === 'RGRGR' || seq7 === 'RGRGR')) {
                patKey = 'ECHO';
                conf = 65;
                pred = 'GREEN';
                found = true;
            }

            // ── ACCELERATION ──
            if (!found && (seq7 === 'GGGGGGG' || seq8 === 'GGGGGGG')) {
                patKey = 'ACCELERATION_B';
                conf = 92;
                pred = 'RED';
                found = true;
            } else if (!found && (seq7 === 'RRRRRRR' || seq8 === 'RRRRRRR')) {
                patKey = 'ACCELERATION_S';
                conf = 92;
                pred = 'GREEN';
                found = true;
            }

            // ── MOMENTUM ──
            if (!found && (seq6 === 'GGGGGG' || seq5 === 'GGGGG')) {
                patKey = 'MOMENTUM_B';
                conf = 90;
                pred = 'RED';
                found = true;
            } else if (!found && (seq6 === 'RRRRRR' || seq5 === 'RRRRR')) {
                patKey = 'MOMENTUM_S';
                conf = 90;
                pred = 'GREEN';
                found = true;
            }

            // ── NOVA ──
            if (!found && (seq6 === 'RGGGGG' || seq5 === 'RGGGG')) {
                patKey = 'NOVA_B';
                conf = 73;
                pred = 'RED';
                found = true;
            } else if (!found && (seq6 === 'GRRRRR' || seq5 === 'GRRRR')) {
                patKey = 'NOVA_S';
                conf = 73;
                pred = 'GREEN';
                found = true;
            }

            // ── REBOUND ──
            if (!found && (seq6 === 'RGGGGG' || seq5 === 'RGGGG')) {
                patKey = 'REBOUND_B';
                conf = 73;
                pred = 'RED';
                found = true;
            } else if (!found && (seq6 === 'GRRRRR' || seq5 === 'GRRRR')) {
                patKey = 'REBOUND_S';
                conf = 73;
                pred = 'GREEN';
                found = true;
            }

            // ── PRERRURE ──
            if (!found && (seq8 === 'GGGGGGGG' || seq7 === 'GGGGGGG')) {
                patKey = 'PRERRURE_B';
                conf = 94;
                pred = 'RED';
                found = true;
            } else if (!found && (seq8 === 'RRRRRRRR' || seq7 === 'RRRRRRR')) {
                patKey = 'PRERRURE_S';
                conf = 94;
                pred = 'GREEN';
                found = true;
            }

            // ── TRIPLE BREAK ──
            if (!found && (seq6 === 'GRGRGR' || seq5 === 'GRGRG')) {
                patKey = 'TRIPLE_BREAK_B';
                conf = 74;
                pred = 'GREEN';
                found = true;
            } else if (!found && (seq6 === 'RGRGRG' || seq5 === 'RGRGR')) {
                patKey = 'TRIPLE_BREAK_S';
                conf = 74;
                pred = 'RED';
                found = true;
            }

            // ── ZIGZAG BLOCK ──
            if (!found && (seq8 === 'GGGRGRGR' || seq7 === 'GGGRGRG')) {
                patKey = 'ZIGZAG_BLOCK_B';
                conf = 81;
                pred = 'RED';
                found = true;
            } else if (!found && (seq8 === 'RRRGRGRG' || seq7 === 'RRRGRGR')) {
                patKey = 'ZIGZAG_BLOCK_S';
                conf = 81;
                pred = 'GREEN';
                found = true;
            }

            // ── HEAVY BIAS ──
            if (!found && b10 >= 7) {
                patKey = 'HEAVY_BIG';
                conf = 82;
                pred = 'RED';
                found = true;
            } else if (!found && b10 <= 3) {
                patKey = 'HEAVY_SMALL';
                conf = 82;
                pred = 'GREEN';
                found = true;
            }

            // ── ZIGZAG ──
            if (!found && (seq6 === 'GRGRGR' || seq5 === 'GRGRG' || seq4 === 'GRGR')) {
                patKey = 'ZIGZAG';
                conf = 74;
                pred = 'GREEN';
                found = true;
            } else if (!found && (seq6 === 'RGRGRG' || seq5 === 'RGRGR' || seq4 === 'RGRG')) {
                patKey = 'ZIGZAG';
                conf = 74;
                pred = 'RED';
                found = true;
            }

            // ── DOUBLE DOUBLE ──
            if (!found && (seq6 === 'GGRRGG' || seq5 === 'GGRRG')) {
                patKey = 'DOUBLE_DOUBLE';
                conf = 83;
                pred = 'RED';
                found = true;
            } else if (!found && (seq6 === 'RRGGRR' || seq5 === 'RRGGR')) {
                patKey = 'DOUBLE_DOUBLE';
                conf = 83;
                pred = 'GREEN';
                found = true;
            }

            // ── Fallback: adaptive ──
            if (!found) {
                const adapt = liveAdaptiveDetect(types);
                if (adapt) {
                    patKey = adapt.method === 'AI_LIVE' ? 'LIVE_LEARN' : 'ADAPTIVE';
                    conf = adapt.conf;
                    pred = adapt.size;
                    found = true;
                }
            }

            // ── Ultimate fallback ──
            if (!found) {
                patKey = 'CHAOS';
                conf = 55;
                pred = Math.random() < 0.5 ? 'GREEN' : 'RED';
            }
        }
    }

    // ── Look up pattern name ──
    const info = BUNNY_PATTERN_NAMES[patKey] || ['Unknown', '🔍 Analyzing...'];
    const patName = info[0];
    let msg = info[1];

    const callCol = pred === 'GREEN' ? '#ef4444' : pred === 'RED' ? '#00d4ff' : '#a855f7';
    const confCol = conf >= 85 ? '#22c55e' : conf >= 70 ? '#facc15' : '#f97316';

    // ── Update DOM ──
    const msgEl = document.getElementById('bunnyMsg');
    if (msgEl) msgEl.innerHTML = msg;

    const patEl = document.getElementById('bunnyPatName');
    if (patEl) { patEl.innerText = patName; }

    const confEl = document.getElementById('bunnyConf');
    if (confEl) { confEl.innerText = conf + '%';
        confEl.style.color = confCol; }

    const callEl = document.getElementById('bunnyCall');
    if (callEl) { callEl.innerText = pred;
        callEl.style.color = callCol; }

    // ── Store for external use ──
    currentPrediction = { size: pred, confidence: conf, pattern: patKey };

    // ── Return result ──
    return {
        pattern: patKey,
        patternName: patName,
        prediction: pred,
        confidence: conf,
        message: msg
    };
}

// ── Export for Node.js / module usage ──
if (typeof module !== 'undefined' && module.exports) {
    module.exports = {
        CPU_PATTERN_DB,
        BUNNY_PATTERN_NAMES,
        runBunnyAI,
        colourToType,
        lookupPattern,
        liveAdaptiveDetect
    };
}

// ── Also attach to window for browser usage ──
if (typeof window !== 'undefined') {
    window.runBunnyAI = runBunnyAI;
    window.CPU_PATTERN_DB = CPU_PATTERN_DB;
    window.BUNNY_PATTERN_NAMES = BUNNY_PATTERN_NAMES;
    window.currentPrediction = currentPrediction;
}

​Your Tasks for Server 3:
​Analyze & Integrate: Understand the CPU_PATTERN_DB and runBunnyAI pattern matching system. Wrap this safely inside a Server 3 controller/module structure so it can be called instantly via an API endpoint or WebSocket.
​Build Custom Number Prediction Logic: My current code only predicts the size (GREEN/RED). I need you to invent a new, highly accurate mathematical function called predictExactNumber(predictedSize, historyArray) that runs immediately after the size prediction.
​Rules for Number Logic:
​If runBunnyAI predicts GREEN: The target number must be randomly or logically chosen from [5, 6, 7, 8, 9].
​If runBunnyAI predicts RED: The target number must be from [0, 1, 2, 3, 4].
​The Algorithm: Do not just guess randomly. Write logic that analyzes the last 1000 numbers in the history array to find the "hot" (most frequent) and "cold" (longest overdue) numbers within the chosen set. Weight the final prediction based on a smart algorithm.
